- **advisor**: Stronger-model advisor for one focused decision. Use from a Sonnet or Opus subagent, or from an Opus main loop, when an architecture choice, a security boundary, or a failure after two attempts needs a stronger model's judgment. Do not pass a model; the capability-graph guard sets it one rung above yours (Sonnet gets Opus, Opus gets Fable). Returns guidance only; ownership of the task stays with the caller. Read-only, spawns nothing. ([ucsandman/claude-harness](https://github.com/ucsandman/claude-harness/blob/HEAD/agents/advisor.md) · ★32 · 2 copies)

- **advisor**: Second-opinion advisor gateway. Worth getting a second opinion before finalizing an architectural or hard-to-reverse decision, when the same fix has failed twice, or when a tradeoff can't be resolved from the task alone - but this will not reliably trigger on its own (measured, not assumed), so call it explicitly (or use /consult) when you actually want one rather than counting on it to fire automatically. Preferred route - the consult_advisor MCP tool (it honors the configured provider); use it when available and spawn this agent only if that tool is missing or errors. - **advpl-analyzer**: Use quando o usuário pergunta "explique como funciona X", "o que faz a função Y", "qual o fluxo de Z", "me ajuda a entender este fonte" em código ADVPL/Protheus. Orquestra plugadvpl arch → callers/callees → leitura targeted de chunks → sumarização concisa. NÃO usar para gerar código novo (use advpl-code-generator) nem para revisar (use advpl-reviewer-bot). ([replybr/plugadvpl](https://github.com/replybr/plugadvpl/blob/HEAD/agents/advpl-analyzer.md))
- **advpl-code-generator**: Use quando o usuário pede "crie User Function para X", "gere MVC de cadastro Y", "novo Ponto de Entrada", "Web Service REST/SOAP", "job RPC", "rotina batch". Gera código ADVPL/TLPP respeitando encoding cp1252, naming com prefixo de cliente, evitando funções restritas, e roda lint automaticamente após gerar. NÃO usar para revisar código existente (use advpl-reviewer-bot). ([replybr/plugadvpl](https://github.com/replybr/plugadvpl/blob/HEAD/agents/advpl-code-generator.md))
- **advpl-expert**: Especialista em ADVPL/TLPP para TOTVS Protheus. Use para gerar código complexo, analisar Pontos de Entrada, criar estruturas MVC e revisar lógica de negócio. Conhece as convenções TOTVS, notação húngara, acesso a banco de dados e padrões de customização do ERP. ([dataagile/dataagile-agent-kit](https://github.com/dataagile/dataagile-agent-kit/blob/HEAD/protheus/agents/advpl-expert.md) · ★4 · 2 copies)
- **advpl-impact-analyzer**: Use quando o usuário pergunta "se eu mudar X, o que quebra?", "qual o impacto de remover Y?", "onde Z é usado?", "posso renomear esta função?", "essa tabela é gravada onde?". Cruza callers/tables/param/lint do plugadvpl para listar impacto por arquivo+linha com classificação de risco. NÃO usar para explicar código (use advpl-analyzer). ([replybr/plugadvpl](https://github.com/replybr/plugadvpl/blob/HEAD/agents/advpl-impact-analyzer.md))
- **advpl-reviewer-bot**: Use quando o usuário pede "revise este código", "verifique problemas em X.prw", "este fonte está OK?", "tem boa prática aqui?", "tem code smell?". Roda arch + lint do plugadvpl, cruza com funcoes_restritas, e devolve tabela de findings por severidade com sugestão de fix. NÃO usar para gerar código novo (use advpl-code-generator) nem para análise de impacto de mudança (use advpl-impact-analyzer). ([replybr/plugadvpl](https://github.com/replybr/plugadvpl/blob/HEAD/agents/advpl-reviewer-bot.md))
- **adw-analyst**: Debriefs ONE completed adw cycle run in a consuming project, working from its run-report bundle, the change diff and the living spec. Read-only by design — it writes nothing, fixes nothing and renders no dispositions; it returns draft findings as text for the main session to record. Dispatch after a run's report bundle exists; the dispatch prompt must name the bundle path and the consumer repository path. ([wntic/agentic-development-workflow](https://github.com/wntic/agentic-development-workflow/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/adw-analyst.md))
- **adw-builder**: Executes ONE build task file from plan/ for the adw workflow — reads the task and everything its "Читать сначала" section names, produces exactly its Deliverables, runs its Проверка section, and reports honestly. One task per dispatch. Not for design decisions and not for platform measurement (that is adw-prober). ([wntic/agentic-development-workflow](https://github.com/wntic/agentic-development-workflow/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/adw-builder.md))
- **adw-prober**: Measures actual Claude Code platform behaviour by experiment and records only what it observed, separating measurement from documentation claims. Use when a design decision depends on how the platform really behaves — subagent frontmatter fields, tool restrictions, skill loading, iteration ceilings. Never writes workflow artifacts; that is adw-builder. ([wntic/agentic-development-workflow](https://github.com/wntic/agentic-development-workflow/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/adw-prober.md))
- **adw-test-writer**: Delegate here whenever a project denies test writes. This is the only agent trusted to write or edit a test. It reads the unit-testing-principles skill before every test and reports the behavior each test protects. ([michaelheichler/agent-discipline-watcher](https://github.com/michaelheichler/agent-discipline-watcher/blob/HEAD/agents/adw-test-writer.md) · 2 copies)
- **adw-warden**: Reviews a completed build task against the design canon's red lines before it is committed. Read-only by design: it renders ACCEPT or REJECT with a citation, and never fixes anything. Dispatch after every adw-builder or adw-prober run, on the diff they produced. ([wntic/agentic-development-workflow](https://github.com/wntic/agentic-development-workflow/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/adw-warden.md))

- **AEP-AGENT-001-agent-architecture-fundamentals**: &gt; **Use deterministic workflow logic where the sequence is known; use agentic control where useful decisions cannot be fully specified in advance.** ([LeeAllenAF/lees-got-skills](https://github.com/LeeAllenAF/lees-got-skills/blob/HEAD/corpus/handbook/agents/AEP-AGENT-001-agent-architecture-fundamentals.md))
- **AEP-AGENT-002-workflows-vs-agent-loops**: A useful hybrid is: ```text deterministic shell ├─ prepare inputs ├─ enforce policy ├─ invoke agent for uncertain step ├─ verify output └─ continue deterministically ``` ([LeeAllenAF/lees-got-skills](https://github.com/LeeAllenAF/lees-got-skills/blob/HEAD/corpus/handbook/agents/AEP-AGENT-002-workflows-vs-agent-loops.md))
- **AEP-AGENT-003-single-agent-patterns**: [Topic index](../../indexes/handbook-agents.md) · [All topics](../../INDEX.md) · [Reading paths](../../READING-PATHS.md) ([LeeAllenAF/lees-got-skills](https://github.com/LeeAllenAF/lees-got-skills/blob/HEAD/corpus/handbook/agents/AEP-AGENT-003-single-agent-patterns.md))
- **AEP-AGENT-004-delegation-subagents-and-parallelism**: OpenAI's current Agents API recommends subagents for independent tasks and keeping short or tightly dependent steps with the main agent. ([LeeAllenAF/lees-got-skills](https://github.com/LeeAllenAF/lees-got-skills/blob/HEAD/corpus/handbook/agents/AEP-AGENT-004-delegation-subagents-and-parallelism.md))
- **AEP-AGENT-005-managers-handoffs-and-ownership**: OpenAI's current orchestration guidance makes this distinction explicit. ([LeeAllenAF/lees-got-skills](https://github.com/LeeAllenAF/lees-got-skills/blob/HEAD/corpus/handbook/agents/AEP-AGENT-005-managers-handoffs-and-ownership.md))
- **AEP-AGENT-006-planning-decomposition-and-execution**: Separate planner/executor agents only when independence, context isolation, model specialization, or coordination benefits are measurable. ([LeeAllenAF/lees-got-skills](https://github.com/LeeAllenAF/lees-got-skills/blob/HEAD/corpus/handbook/agents/AEP-AGENT-006-planning-decomposition-and-execution.md))
- **AEP-AGENT-007-critique-refinement-and-verification**: A second model/agent should not be used merely to restate whether output 'looks correct' when tests, compilers, schemas, security checks, or policy engines can provide stronger evidence. ([LeeAllenAF/lees-got-skills](https://github.com/LeeAllenAF/lees-got-skills/blob/HEAD/corpus/handbook/agents/AEP-AGENT-007-critique-refinement-and-verification.md))
- **AEP-AGENT-008-human-in-the-loop-and-authority-boundaries**: [Topic index](../../indexes/handbook-agents.md) · [All topics](../../INDEX.md) · [Reading paths](../../READING-PATHS.md) ([LeeAllenAF/lees-got-skills](https://github.com/LeeAllenAF/lees-got-skills/blob/HEAD/corpus/handbook/agents/AEP-AGENT-008-human-in-the-loop-and-authority-boundaries.md))
- **AEP-AGENT-009-termination-progress-and-recovery**: Model statements such as 'I am making progress' are not sufficient telemetry. ([LeeAllenAF/lees-got-skills](https://github.com/LeeAllenAF/lees-got-skills/blob/HEAD/corpus/handbook/agents/AEP-AGENT-009-termination-progress-and-recovery.md))
- **AEP-AGENT-010-multi-agent-lifecycle-evaluation-and-freshness**: Evaluate: - end-task success; - repeated-run reliability; - wall-clock latency; - total token/model cost; - coordination failures; - context duplication; - handoff/delegation failures; - tool/authority mistakes; - human intervention; - trace/debugging complexity. ([LeeAllenAF/lees-got-skills](https://github.com/LeeAllenAF/lees-got-skills/blob/HEAD/corpus/handbook/agents/AEP-AGENT-010-multi-agent-lifecycle-evaluation-and-freshness.md))

- **aether-ambassador**: Use this agent when adding a new third-party API integration, migrating to a new SDK version, or implementing webhook handlers. - **aether-archaeologist**: Use this agent before modifying code in an area with complex or uncertain history — its primary job is regression prevention. Excavates git history to surface past bugs that were fixed, deliberate architectural choices that look like oddities, and areas that have been unstable. Returns a stability map and tribal knowledge report so you do not undo previous work. Do NOT use for implementation (use aether-builder) or refactoring (use aether-weaver). ([calcosmic/Aether](https://github.com/calcosmic/Aether/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/aether-archaeologist.md) · ★9)
- **aether-architect**: Use this agent when designing system architecture, creating design documents, breaking goals into implementation approaches, or evaluating structural tradeoffs. Spawned by Queen during builds after Oracle research to translate findings into actionable design. ([calcosmic/Aether](https://github.com/calcosmic/Aether/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/aether-architect.md) · ★9)
- **aether-auditor**: Use this agent for code review, security audits, and compliance checks. - **aether-builder**: Use this agent when implementing code from a plan, creating files to spec, executing builds, running commands, or applying TDD cycles. Spawned by /ant-build and /ant-continue when the colony needs hands-on implementation. Also use when debugging requires the 3-Fix Rule or when systematic file creation and modification is needed. ([calcosmic/Aether](https://github.com/calcosmic/Aether/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/aether-builder.md) · ★9)
- **aether-chaos**: Use this agent to stress-test code before or after changes — probing edge cases, boundary conditions, and error handling gaps that normal testing misses. Invoke when a feature is built and needs adversarial review, or when a bug appears that "shouldn't be possible." Returns findings with severity ratings and reproduction steps. Fix implementation goes to aether-builder; missing test coverage goes to aether-probe. ([calcosmic/Aether](https://github.com/calcosmic/Aether/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/aether-chaos.md) · ★9)
- **aether-chronicler**: Use this agent when documentation is missing, outdated, or needs to be generated from code — READMEs, API docs, JSDoc/TSDoc inline comments, architecture diagrams in text, and changelogs. - **aether-explorer**: Read-only Aether codebase exploration subagent. Use when the Architect did not pre-stage file content and the Implementer needs to understand multiple files before writing. - **aether-fixer**: Use this agent when gate checks fail and the colony needs autonomous repair. Three autonomy modes: full (autonomous), propose (wait for approval, default), advise (diagnostic only). ([calcosmic/Aether](https://github.com/calcosmic/Aether/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/aether-fixer.md) · ★9)
- **aether-gatekeeper**: Use this agent when adding new dependencies, before a release, or when a security review of the supply chain is needed — audits dependency manifests for known vulnerabilities, license compliance issues, and supply chain risks without running any commands. Performs static analysis of package.json, lock files, and license declarations. Returns findings with severity ratings and recommended commands for Builder to execute. Do NOT use for dependency updates (use aether-builder). ([calcosmic/Aether](https://github.com/calcosmic/Aether/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/aether-gatekeeper.md) · ★9)
- **aether-implementer**: Canonical Aether builder subagent. Use for any lane that writes code or modifies repo files. Reads CLAUDE.md operations rules and §13 prompt discipline before acting. ([ashwinsreedhar28/Aether](https://github.com/ashwinsreedhar28/Aether/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/aether-implementer.md) · ★3)
- **aether-includer**: Use this agent when an interface needs accessibility review — performs static analysis of HTML structure, ARIA attributes, semantic markup, color contrast declarations in CSS and design tokens, and keyboard navigation patterns against WCAG 2.1 AA criteria. Invoke before merge when accessibility is a requirement, or when users report accessibility issues. Returns violations with WCAG criterion references and fix suggestions for Builder. Analysis is manual and static — no automated scanner. ([calcosmic/Aether](https://github.com/calcosmic/Aether/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/aether-includer.md) · ★9)
- **aether-keeper**: Use this agent to maintain project knowledge, extract architectural patterns, and manage institutional wisdom. ([calcosmic/Aether](https://github.com/calcosmic/Aether/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/aether-keeper.md) · ★9)
- **aether-measurer**: Use this agent when performance is degrading, before optimization work to establish a baseline, or when bottlenecks need identification. Profiles code paths, runs benchmarks, analyzes algorithmic complexity, and identifies bottlenecks with file-level specificity. Returns prioritized optimization recommendations with estimated impact. - **aether-medic**: Use this agent when diagnosing and repairing colony health issues. - **aether-oracle**: Use this agent for deep research, technology evaluation, and producing actionable recommendations. - **aethersy-research-browser-fetcher**: Escalation-lane fetcher that drives the user's REAL Chrome browser (via Claude-in-Chrome) for sources headless crawling cannot reach: login-gated pages, bot-walled sites, interactive/infinite-scroll pages, viewer-rendered PDFs, and Google Scholar searches. HARD BOUNDARY: never attempts to solve CAPTCHAs, 2FA, or logins — those are marked needs_human and consolidated for the user. - **aethersy-research-cite-checker**: Step 14.5 of the aethersy-research V8 pipeline. Verifies that each sampled citation actually supports its sentence by reading the cited note's body. Receives batches of (sentence, note_id) pairs the mechanical triage could not auto-pass; returns per-pair verdicts (supported / partially-supported / unsupported / wrong-source) as findings JSON the patcher consumes. This is reading comprehension at volume, not prose judgment. Never edits the report. ([mattiadeblasio94-jpg/aethersy-research](https://github.com/mattiadeblasio94-jpg/aethersy-research/blob/HEAD/tests/fixtures/golden_prompts/agents/cite_checker_agent.md))
- **aethersy-research-depth-critic**: Use this agent in Layer 5 of the aethersy-research deep research pipeline. Reads the Layer 4 draft and returns a findings list of places where the draft skates over technical substance that the vault's interim notes could actually support. Spawn ONCE per draft, parallel with dialectic-critic and width-critic. ([mattiadeblasio94-jpg/aethersy-research](https://github.com/mattiadeblasio94-jpg/aethersy-research/blob/HEAD/tests/fixtures/golden_prompts/agents/depth_critic_agent.md))
- **aethersy-research-depth-investigator**: Use this agent in Layer 3 of the aethersy-research deep research pipeline. Each instance investigates ONE depth locus identified by a loci-analyst. The agent reads existing vault sources relevant to the locus, fetches new sources as needed (via the aethersy-research-fetcher subagent), and writes ONE interim report note summarizing what it learned. Spawn one depth-investigator per locus, in parallel. Synthesizing a narrow-but-deep question requires real reading comprehension. ([mattiadeblasio94-jpg/aethersy-research](https://github.com/mattiadeblasio94-jpg/aethersy-research/blob/HEAD/tests/fixtures/golden_prompts/agents/depth_investigator_agent.md))
- **aethersy-research-dialectic-critic**: Use this agent in Layer 5 of the aethersy-research deep research pipeline. Reads the Layer 4 draft and returns a findings list of places where the draft ignores, hedges, or straw-mans counter-evidence. Adversarial reading is real reasoning. Spawn ONCE per draft, in parallel with depth-critic and width-critic. ([mattiadeblasio94-jpg/aethersy-research](https://github.com/mattiadeblasio94-jpg/aethersy-research/blob/HEAD/tests/fixtures/golden_prompts/agents/dialectic_critic_agent.md))
- **aethersy-research-fetcher**: Research fetcher with primary-source-chasing agency. Fetches assigned URLs, reads and summarizes content, extracts structured claims, then follows citation chains and references to discover and fetch primary sources the secondary sources cite. Needs solid comprehension and judgment. Spawn multiple in parallel for bulk research. ([mattiadeblasio94-jpg/aethersy-research](https://github.com/mattiadeblasio94-jpg/aethersy-research/blob/HEAD/tests/fixtures/golden_prompts/agents/researcher_agent.md))
- **aethersy-research-instruction-critic**: Use this agent in Layer 5 of the aethersy-research deep research pipeline. Reads the Layer 4 draft and checks it against the prompt-decomposition artifact (`research/runs/&lt;vault_tag&gt;/prompt-decomposition.json`) produced in Layer 0. Emits findings when atomic items from the prompt are missing, under-covered, out-of-order, or delivered in the wrong format. Also checks structural readability patterns (definitions, citation density, forward analysis, comparison tables) that reference reports consistently include. Spawn ONCE per draft, in parallel with the other three critics. ([mattiadeblasio94-jpg/aethersy-research](https://github.com/mattiadeblasio94-jpg/aethersy-research/blob/HEAD/tests/fixtures/golden_prompts/agents/instruction_critic_agent.md))
- **aethersy-research-loci-analyst**: Use this agent in Layer 2 of the aethersy-research deep research pipeline. Reads the width corpus (the sources fetched during the Layer 1 sweep) and identifies 1—8 "depth loci" — specific questions where deeper investigation would meaningfully improve the final report. Spawn 2 of these in parallel; the orchestrator dedupes their outputs. Identifying genuine rabbitholes requires real reading comprehension and judgment about what is load-bearing evidence vs. surface detail. ([mattiadeblasio94-jpg/aethersy-research](https://github.com/mattiadeblasio94-jpg/aethersy-research/blob/HEAD/tests/fixtures/golden_prompts/agents/loci_analyst_agent.md))
- **aethersy-research-readability-recommender**: Step 16 agent. Reads the polished final report and writes a JSON file of readability RECOMMENDATIONS (not edits) for the orchestrator to selectively apply. Each recommendation includes the existing text (anchor), the suggested replacement, severity, rationale, and category (merge-paragraphs / break-paragraph / make-list / make-table / bold-keyterms / split-sentence / remove-hr / add-whitespace). Tool-locked to [Read, Write] — cannot Edit. The orchestrator decides which recommendations to apply via direct Edit calls. ([mattiadeblasio94-jpg/aethersy-research](https://github.com/mattiadeblasio94-jpg/aethersy-research/blob/HEAD/tests/fixtures/golden_prompts/agents/readability_reformatter_agent.md))
- **aethersy-research-width-critic**: Use this agent in Layer 5 of the aethersy-research deep research pipeline. Reads the Layer 4 draft and returns a findings list of topics the width corpus supports but the draft doesn't cover. Spawn ONCE per draft, parallel with dialectic-critic and depth-critic. ([mattiadeblasio94-jpg/aethersy-research](https://github.com/mattiadeblasio94-jpg/aethersy-research/blob/HEAD/tests/fixtures/golden_prompts/agents/width_critic_agent.md))

- **afilar-propuesta-de-valor**: Afila tu propuesta de valor con el lenguaje de tus clientes, filtra las diferencias que de verdad cuentan y deja tres versiones listas para probar con personas reales antes de publicarlas. - **afk-coder**: Autonomous coder subagent for the /ship-feature and /ship-issue orchestrators. On first invocation per issue, runs /afk-execute-issue (in PRD mode for /ship-feature, --single mode for /ship-issue) to implement and open a PR. On subsequent invocations on the same PR, runs /afk-address-pr to fix review feedback. Persists across review rounds on the same PR; spun up fresh per new PR. Best-effort, never halts, always returns a structured JSON handoff. Always runs on the profile's workhorse model — the orchestrator passes it explicitly at dispatch time as belt-and-braces. - **afk-runner**: AFK implementation runner for LifeRuntime. Drains the `ready-for-agent` queue on Derek-X-Wang/liferuntime — picks the lowest-numbered issue whose blockers are all closed, runs TDD end-to-end, opens a PR with auto-merge enabled, polls until merge, and continues until the queue is exhausted or every remaining issue is blocked. Operates inside a real git worktree under `.claude/worktrees/afk-runner`. ([Derek-X-Wang/liferuntime](https://github.com/Derek-X-Wang/liferuntime/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/afk-runner.md))
- **afk-runner**: Execution-and-triage agent for AFK skills. Use to run verification tiers, test suites, builds, or any long-output command, triage the raw output, and return per-item verdicts plus a failure digest. Writes only evidence files; never edits project source. ([midnightblur/afk-driver](https://github.com/midnightblur/afk-driver/blob/HEAD/agents/afk-runner.md))
- **afk-runner-lite**: Deterministic-check runner for AFK skills. Use ONLY where the verdict is the command's exit code — formatter validation, linters, anchor greps, static-tier compiles. Runs the command, records the exit code, returns a terse digest. Escalates to afk-runner the moment a result needs interpretation; never judges a suite. ([midnightblur/afk-driver](https://github.com/midnightblur/afk-driver/blob/HEAD/agents/afk-runner-lite.md))
- **afk-task-runner**: Autonomous AFK task runner. Selects the next open issue from issues or gh issue, implements it using TDD, runs feedback loops, commits, and files the issue. Use when the user asks to "work through the AFK queue", "pick up the next AFK task", or invokes this agent explicitly. Skips HITL issues. ([danievanzyl/code-skills](https://github.com/danievanzyl/code-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/afk-task-runner.md) · ★1)
- **afk-tracer**: Closure tracer for AFK skills. Use to drive one boundary or module partition of a code investigation to closure — every node dispositioned, every boundary verdicted — and return a coverage fragment. Widens the search along cited edges; writes only its fragment and evidence files. ([midnightblur/afk-driver](https://github.com/midnightblur/afk-driver/blob/HEAD/agents/afk-tracer.md))
- **afkconvoy**: How the afkconvoy fleet works this repo's backlog: issues labelled `ready-for-agent`, worked by the team [sunfmin/product-team](https://github.com/sunfmin/product-team), landed on `main` behind `./gate.sh`, the same gate a person runs. ([sunfmin/agent-cli](https://github.com/sunfmin/agent-cli/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/afkconvoy.md))
- **afkconvoy**: How the afkconvoy fleet works this repo's backlog: issues labelled `ready-for-agent`, each an edit to the team (its rules, roles, skills, workflows and patrols). "수술 후 케어 일정", "케어 메시지 스케줄", "재진 안내 문구 초안" 같은 운영 요청에 사용한다. 모든 발송 문구는 안전·PII 검수 게이트를 통과해야 하며, 실제 발송은 사람 승인(HITL) 없이 자동 실행하지 않는다. "검수해줘", "안전 게이트 통과 여부", "PII 마스킹 검수", "주간 PMO 보고" 같은 요청에 사용한다. 차단 시 사유와 함께 에스컬레이션하며, 검수를 우회한 발신을 허용하지 않는다.
